Result: 在MMSearch-Plus数据集上对四个前沿多模态模型的实验表明，表面准确率显著高于实际轨迹正确率，且沉默失败具有能力依赖性，常随能力提升而转移而非消失。

Conclusion: 多模态代理系统的可靠性不能仅靠答案准确率衡量，需引入轨迹级评估以揭示沉默失败，未来系统设计应关注证据链的可信性。

Abstract: Multimodal agentic search systems increasingly rely on external tools to answer knowledge-intensive visual questions. However, existing evaluations mainly focus on final-answer accuracy and may miss failures in the search trajectory. In this work, we study such hidden reliability issues as silent failures. We introduce a six-category taxonomy covering modality shortcuts, phantom grounding, wrong-evidence-right-answer cases, over-retrieval laundering, cross-modal contradiction, and provenance hallucination. Based on this taxonomy, we build a trajectory-level diagnostic pipeline that evaluates both answer correctness and evidence-grounding quality under a unified ReAct-style scaffold. Experiments on MMSearch-Plus trajectories across four frontier multimodal models show that surface accuracy consistently overestimates true trajectory-level correctness. We further use cross-judge validation, blank-image stress tests, and tool ablations to show that silent failures are capability-dependent and often shift rather than disappear. Home-page: https://github.com/DingWu1021/silent-failures-multimodal-agentic-search

</details>


### [149] [Rewarding Better Thinking for LLM Preference Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19824)
*Xubo Liu,Wenya Guo,Ruxue Yan,Xinying Qian,Ying Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向推理过程的奖励机制TCR（Thinking Checklist Reward），用于增强基于强化学习的大模型偏好对齐，通过构建样本特定的思维清单并结合EMA残差形式，提升对推理轨迹的细粒度监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的偏好对齐方法多依赖结果级奖励信号，难以对推理过程中的细粒度偏好进行有效建模，导致信用分配粗糙、轨迹级偏好未被充分定义。

Method: 提出Thinking Checklist Reward（TCR），将偏好样本对转化为样本特定的思考清单，用于评估生成推理链是否涵盖应考虑的关键点；引入指数移动平均（EMA）残差形式，分离出超出结果奖励可预测部分的思维冗余，以减少与结果级监督的重叠。

Result: 在来自三个模型家族的五个模型上实验表明，TCR在多个多样化基准上持续提升了偏好对齐性能，消融实验验证了EMA残差机制和样本特定清单监督的重要性。

Conclusion: TCR是一种有效的过程导向奖励机制，能够细化信用分配，显式建模推理过程中的偏好，从而提升大语言模型的偏好对齐效果。

Abstract: LLM preference alignment aims to optimize models toward human preferences across diverse user instructions. Reinforcement learning has become a major post-training approach for this goal, but existing proxy rewards are often outcome-level, mainly evaluating the final response while providing limited guidance for the reasoning trajectory. This can make credit assignment coarse when multiple responses receive similar final scores, leaving trajectory-level preferences under-specified. To address this limitation, we propose Thinking Checklist Reward (TCR), a process-oriented reward for RL-based preference alignment. TCR converts preference pairs into sample-specific thinking checklists and uses them to evaluate whether the generated reasoning trace addresses the preference-implied considerations. To reduce overlap with outcome-level supervision, TCR further introduces an exponential moving average (EMA) residual formulation to isolate a complementary thinking surplus beyond what is predictable from the outcome reward. Experiments on five models from three model families show that TCR consistently improves alignment performance across diverse benchmarks, with ablations further validating the importance of EMA-based residual formulation and sample-specific checklist supervision.

</details>


### [150] [Know Your Agent: Reconnaissance-Driven Pentesting of AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19837)
*Or Zion Eliav,Eyal Lenga,Shir Bernstien,Yisroel Mirsky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为Know Your Agent (KYA)的框架，用于自动化黑盒、基于侦察的AI代理渗透测试，通过探测代理、构建目标画像并利用这些画像设计更强攻击，以揭示间接提示注入漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统渗透测试在每一步都使用侦察来发现未知弱点并加强攻击，而当前AI代理缺乏类似的系统性侦察方法，导致难以充分评估其安全性，尤其是面对间接提示注入等复杂攻击时。

Method: 通过形式化代理侦察过程，建模其所需获取的知识资产（如代理的行为特征、依赖组件和上下文处理方式），并设计KYA框架实现自动化侦察：包括探测代理响应、构建目标配置文件，并基于该配置文件生成针对性的攻击提示。

Result: KYA在多个代理安全基准和一个真实世界的编码代理上进行了评估，成功发现了现有代理中的多个漏洞，并展示了比传统方法更强的攻击能力；同时作者开源了KYA框架、相关基准和基线实现。

Conclusion: AI代理需要类似传统渗透测试的系统性侦察机制，KYA验证了侦察驱动的方法能有效提升对间接提示注入等威胁的检测与利用能力，为未来AI系统安全评估提供了可复现的新范式。

Abstract: Traditional pentesting uses reconnaissance at each step to uncover unseen weaknesses, build stronger attacks, and advance the objective; we argue that AI agents require the same treatment. We formalize agent reconnaissance by modeling the process and identifying the knowledge assets it seeks to extract: what they are, how they are used, and which agent weaknesses they exploit to give adversaries leverage in indirect prompt injection attacks. We instantiate these insights in Know Your Agent (KYA), a framework that automates black-box, reconnaissance-driven pentesting by probing agents, building target profiles, and using those profiles to craft stronger attacks. We evaluate KYA on agent-security benchmarks and a real-world coding agent, and release KYA, its benchmarks, and baseline implementations for reproducibility.

</details>


### [151] [DocOps: A Verifiable Benchmark for Autonomous Agents in Complex Document Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19865)
*Jiazhen Jiang,Boxi Cao,Lingyong Yan,Yaojie Lu,Hongyu Lin,Shuaiqiang Wang,Dawei Yin,Xianpei Han,Le Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了DocOps，一个用于评估自主智能体在处理数字文档时性能的可验证框架，并揭示了现有模型在复杂、长距离任务中的关键缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自主智能体的发展，其操作数字文档的能力对实现通用AI助手和自动化工作流至关重要。然而当前缺乏系统性评估方法来衡量智能体在真实场景下的文档操作能力。

Method: 提出DocOps框架，基于现实实践构建层次化分类体系，将文档操作分解为原子维度和递进式工作流复杂度，并在多种代理架构中对闭源和开源模型进行系统评估。

Result: 发现即使是前沿的智能体配置在处理高度耦合、长距离依赖的任务时仍存在显著局限性；细粒度分析揭示出三大失败模式：长期状态跟踪崩溃、浅层语义验证以及结构性元数据的破坏性编辑。

Conclusion: 当前智能体难以维持全局文档一致性，需面向非破坏性、强健性方向重新设计以适应复杂数字生态系统。

Abstract: As autonomous agents rapidly evolve, their ability to reliably manipulate ubiquitous digital documents has become critical for enabling general-purpose AI assistants and automating complex workspace workflows. In this paper, we introduce DocOps, a deterministically verifiable evaluation framework underpinned by a hierarchical taxonomy that deconstructs document operations inspired by real-world practices into atomic dimensions and escalating workflow complexities. Based on DocOps, we systematically evaluate representative closed- and open-source models across various agentic harnesses, revealing that even the most advanced frontier configurations still exhibit profound limitations when handling highly coupled, long-range tasks. Furthermore, a fine-grained analysis of existing agents' manipulation behaviors uncovers 3 key failure modes: long-term state tracking collapse, shallow semantic verification, and destructive editing of structural metadata. Ultimately, our work exposes the capability boundaries of agents in maintaining global document consistency, shedding light on the future design of robust, non-destructive agents for complex digital ecosystems.

</details>


### [152] [JANUS: Foreseeing Latent Risk for Long-Horizon Agent Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19913)
*Yuan Xiong,Linji Hao,Shizhu He,Yequan Wang,Lijun Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Janus是一个面向长周期代理安全的前瞻性框架，通过多智能体模拟生成多样化轨迹，并训练具备预测和裁决双重任务的守卫模型Vanguard，以在执行前阻止不安全行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着代理系统从内容审核转向预防工具使用中的操作失败，亟需一种能够提前预见延迟风险的安全机制，以应对长周期任务中潜在的复杂安全隐患。

Method: 提出Janus框架，利用多智能体模拟合成代理轨迹，训练共享策略的守卫模型，包含对未来安全相关事件的预测任务和基于当前前缀与预测未来的联合裁决任务，并采用CoAA-RL方法联合优化两个任务。

Result: 在四个代理安全基准测试中，Vanguard相比基线守卫模型平均将保护率提高了15.9个百分点，同时良性任务完成率提升了5.1个百分点。

Conclusion: Janus框架通过结合前瞻性预测与联合决策，有效提升了长周期代理系统的安全性与可用性，为构建具备风险预判能力的智能代理提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Agent safety is moving from content moderation toward preventing operational failures before tool-using agents act. We propose Janus, a foresight-oriented framework for long-horizon agent safety that trains guards to anticipate delayed risks from partial trajectories. Janus synthesizes diverse agent trajectories via multi-agent simulation and learns a shared policy with two coupled tasks: an anticipation task that forecasts safety-relevant futures and an adjudication task that decides safety from both the observed prefix and anticipated future. The two tasks are jointly optimized with CoAA-RL, which rewards forecasts by their utility for downstream safety judgment. The resulting guard model, Vanguard, blocks unsafe actions before execution. Across four agent-safety benchmarks, Vanguard improves average protection by 15.9 percentage points over baseline guards while increasing benign task completion by 5.1 percentage points.

</details>


### [153] [Long-Term Sequential Decision Making under Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19914)
*Irmaan,Mirzanejad,Nadjet Bourdache,Abdel-Illah Mouaddib*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为ERQDP的枚举自由和采样自由的方法，用于在基于根的风险目标下进行有限时域MDP规划，能够有效处理非线性回报分布并提供认证解或明确的残差间隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的动态规划方法在处理非线性的基于根的风险目标时由于破坏了贝尔曼最优性而变得不可行，因此需要一种新的方法来解决这一问题。

Method: 提出了ERQDP方法，该方法通过精确的动态规划解决秩-分位数替代问题，在离散化的回报网格上使用返回概率质量函数(PMFs)对候选策略进行精确评估，并在一个随时可中断的循环中细化替代模型。

Result: 实验结果表明，ERQDP能够在测试基准上返回已认证的解决方案或明确的剩余差距，实现快速的风险参数扫描，并支持风险规避和风险寻求行为。

Conclusion: ERQDP为有限时域MDP规划中的非线性风险目标优化提供了一个有效的解决方案，具有较高的实用价值。

Abstract: We study finite-horizon MDP planning under \emph{root-based} (resolute) risk objectives that apply a rank-dependent functional to the distribution of total returns. Such objectives are non-linear in the return distribution and generally break Bellman optimality, so direct optimization by scenario-tree enumeration is intractable. We propose \textbf{ERQDP}, an enumeration-free and sampling-free method that solves a rank--quantile surrogate via exact DP (Dynamic Programming), evaluates candidate policies exactly by DP over return Probability Mass Functions (PMFs) on a discretized return grid (with an explicit rounding bound), and refines the surrogate in an anytime loop that reports an explicit upper--lower gap (certificate) for the target objective up to discretization budgets. Across tested benchmarks, ERQDP returns certified solutions or explicit residual gaps, enables fast risk-parameter sweeps with substantial runtime gains, and supports both risk-averse and risk-seeking behaviors.

</details>


### [154] [MOF-Sleuth: Tool-Grounded Reward Alignment for Explainable Fine-Grained MOF CIF Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19935)
*Yu Liu,Zhiwei Yang,Diandian Guo,Kun Peng,Fangfang Yuan,Cong Cao,Chaozhuo Li,Zhiyuan Ma,Yanbing Liu,Guobin Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MOF-Sleuth，一种基于强化学习引导的CIF审计代理，通过结合确定性取证模块与推理引擎，实现对金属有机框架（MOF）结构中细微化学和结构错误的可解释性诊断，提升了检测准确性与结果的化学可追溯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MOF数据库中的晶体信息文件（CIF）常含有难以察觉的化学与结构错误，且当前机器学习模型或验证工具缺乏细粒度归因与证据支持的解释能力，导致下游模拟与筛选结果不可靠。因此需要一种能够提供事实依据、细粒度诊断的方法。

Method: 设计MOF-Sleuth系统，包含两个模块：（1）法医实验室（Forensic Lab），用于从CIF文件中提取组成、几何、连接性、占据率、配位和电荷等化学证据；（2）Sleuth推理引擎，利用这些证据生成有据可依的解释、错误类型判断与二元决策。采用奖励引导的强化学习，将工具测量转化为化学解释层面的监督信号，奖励正确的证据引用与推理过程。

Result: 在四个基准测试中，MOF-Sleuth在LLM-based方法和专用MOF机器学习模型中均达到最先进性能，显著提升错误检测、归因能力和解释的化学可追溯性。提出的新指标Chem-GD能有效评估诊断是否基于真实且相关的CIF证据。

Conclusion: MOF-Sleuth通过紧密耦合化学证据提取与语言模型推理，实现了对MOF结构错误的高可信度、可解释性审计，为材料科学中的自动化质量控制提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large metal-organic framework (MOF) databases support simulation, screening, and machine learning through crystallographic information files (CIFs). Subtle chemical and structural errors in these inputs can compromise downstream results and hinder manual inspection. LLM advances in computational chemistry offer paths beyond predictive screening toward fine-grained diagnosis with evidence-grounded explanations. However, two challenges remain: (i) limited fine-grained attribution: MOF-specific validators and machine-learning models scale detection but provide fixed checks, readiness scores, or coarse labels rather than evidence-grounded explanations; and (ii) unreliable CIF reasoning: direct LLM auditing is costly and unreliable because chemical evidence is implicit across atom-site records and requires geometric, connectivity, occupancy, and charge calculations. Both stem from weak coupling between chemical evidence and language-model explanation. We introduce MOF-Sleuth, a reinforcement-guided CIF auditing agent with two modules: a deterministic Forensic Lab and a Sleuth reasoning engine. The Lab derives composition, geometry, connectivity, occupancy, coordination, and charge evidence, and Sleuth uses this evidence to produce an evidence-grounded explanation, error types, and a binary decision. Reward-guided reinforcement learning (RL) turns tool measurements into chemical explanation-level supervision, rewarding not only the final answer but also cited chemical evidence and evidence-supported diagnoses. We introduce Chemically Grounded Diagnosis (Chem-GD), a metric that assesses whether a correct diagnosis is explained by factual, relevant CIF-derived evidence. Across four benchmarks, MOF-Sleuth establishes state-of-the-art performance among LLM-based approaches and MOF-specific machine-learning methods, demonstrating gains in detection, attribution, and grounded explanation quality.

</details>


### [155] [SenWorld: A Digital-Twin Simulation for Generating Context-Rich Evaluation Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19949)
*Zenghui Zhou,Xiaoyang Li,Xiaoxuan Qiao,Zhilang Wei,Tianming Lei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SenWorld是一个基于真实数据构建的数字孪生仿真系统，用于生成带有天然真值标签的智能手机助手评估数据，解决了隐私敏感和标注困难的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估智能手机个人助手需要丰富的上下文数据和已知正确答案，但真实设备数据因隐私问题难以共享，且后验标注或大语言模型评判存在局限。

Method: 提出SenWorld，一种物理锚定、确定性、事件驱动的数字孪生模拟系统，通过构建包含真实地图、天气、节假日和网络数据的虚拟世界，让虚拟人物经历完整一天，自动生成全系统快照和带指针标签的评估用例。

Result: 在16个北京虚拟人物上验证，生成数据在类别分布（JSD 0.070）和通信节律（JSD < 0.1）上接近真实用户基准，虽记录较短；生成717个评估案例，发现生产级助手78个错误，集中于通话和短信记录检索失败，所有错误均由快照指针确认为助手端检索错误。

Conclusion: SenWorld提供了一条隐私安全、可复现、分布可控的评估数据生成路径，其标签由构造过程固定，无需依赖后验标注或大语言模型评判。

Abstract: Smartphone personal assistants reason over longitudinal personal data, yet evaluating them requires context-rich evaluation data whose correct answers are known, and real device traces are too privacy-sensitive to share. To address this challenge, we present SenWorld, a physically grounded, deterministic, event-sourced digital-twin simulation that generates such data with ground truth fixed by construction. In SenWorld, personas live through a full day in a world built from real map, weather, holiday, and network data; every observable signal is archived in full-system snapshots; and each evaluation case is labeled by a pointer to an existing record rather than by post-hoc annotation or a large language model (LLM) judge. We evaluate this method with 16 personas in Beijing. The generated data closely matches the held-out real-user benchmark in category distribution (Jensen--Shannon divergence (JSD) 0.070) and in the daily rhythm of communication records (JSD below 0.1), though generated records remain shorter than real ones. Without scripted interaction, personas form a fully reciprocated dialogue subgraph and differentiated behavioral repertoires. Projected into 717 evaluation cases, the generated data exposes 78 failures in a production smartphone assistant, concentrating on call and Short Message Service (SMS) records while contacts, schedules, and alarms never fail. The snapshot pointer confirms each failure as an assistant-side retrieval error, with no LLM judge involved. Overall, SenWorld offers a privacy-safe, reproducible, and distribution-checked path to evaluation data whose labels are fixed by construction.

</details>


### [156] [EvoThink: Evolving Thinking in Large Reasoning Models via Self-Pruning and Aha-Moment Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19962)
*Xinbang Dai,Zheyu Xin,Huikang Hu,Lin Ren,Rihui Jin,Guohui Xiao,Guilin Qi,Kuicai Dong,Zhaocheng Du,Yuyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EvoThink是一种旨在减少大推理模型（LRM）中冗余验证步骤并提升推理效率与能力的框架，包含自修剪训练和顿悟时刻偏好优化两个核心组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以精细区分有益和冗余的推理步骤，导致在追求效率的同时可能损害推理能力，因此需要一种既能精简过程又能增强推理的新方法。

Method: 提出EvoThink框架，包括无监督的自修剪训练（SPT），用于迭代剪枝冗余步骤并基于简洁轨迹自训练；以及受遗传算法启发的顿悟时刻偏好优化（AMPO），通过识别有价值的失败推理尝试，合成从错误到正确的‘顿悟’数据来优化模型。

Result: 在数学推理和代码生成基准上的广泛评估表明，EvoThink显著减少了推理时的token使用量，同时提升了LRM的推理能力。

Conclusion: EvoThink能有效平衡推理效率与能力，通过精细化管理推理路径，在降低冗余的同时增强模型性能。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) often suffer from overthinking due to redundant verification steps. Existing approaches for mitigating overthinking, such as fast-slow thinking switching and reasoning trajectory compression, fail to make a fine-grained distinction between beneficial and redundant steps within the LRM's reasoning process, and may thus impair reasoning capability in their pursuit of efficiency. To simultaneously improve reasoning efficiency and capability, we propose EvoThink, a framework that reduces redundant verification and encourages the exploration of new reasoning paths. EvoThink comprises two key components: Self-Pruning Training (SPT), an unsupervised method that iteratively prunes redundant reasoning steps and self-trains on the concise trajectories; and Aha-Moment Preference Optimization (AMPO), which, inspired by genetic algorithms, identifies valuable failed reasoning attempts, synthesizes from-wrong-to-right aha-moment data, and optimizes the model to internalize this reasoning pattern. Extensive evaluations across mathematical reasoning and code generation benchmarks demonstrate that EvoThink not only substantially reduces inference-time token usage but also improves the reasoning capability of LRMs.

</details>


### [157] [The Giant Hippocampus: From Structural Monoculture to a System of Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19973)
*Jaeho Seol*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文认为当前AI领域过度依赖统一的Transformer架构是一种结构性错误，主张借鉴大脑皮层的异质性结构，提出一种具有不同模块和标准化接口的异构拓扑网络作为替代设计范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指出AI模型盲目模仿大脑“通用性”而忽视其结构多样性的问题，强调现有架构如Transformer并不适合作为通用认知模型，并试图纠正将单一架构广泛应用于不匹配任务的设计偏差。

Method: 通过对比神经科学中的细胞构筑研究与深度学习架构发展史，分析卷积神经网络成功的原因及其被抛弃的历史偶然性（硬件彩票），并从功能主义角度将Transformer类比为海马体而非全脑皮层，进而提出异构拓扑网络的设计原则。

Result: 论证了使用单一重复结构（如Transformer）无法有效模拟多样化认知功能；揭示Mixture-of-Experts等方法并未真正实现结构多样性；提出了一个保持各模块归纳偏置并通过标准接口通信的系统级架构方案。

Conclusion: AI架构设计应以结构证据为指导，在训练前明确模块化分工，构建具备功能性多样性的系统，而不是依赖统一架构的规模化复制。

Abstract: AI researchers describe state-of-the-art models as one thing repeated at scale: the Transformer, wired identically for text, pixels, or speech. Neuroscientists describe the cortex as a mosaic - dense Layer 4 in visual cortex for spatial encoding, thick Layers 5/6 in motion cortex for temporal integration - different jobs solved by different structures. This paper argues the gap is a structural error, not a stylistic one, and is measurable. A century of cytoarchitecture, from Brodmann to single-cell Patch-seq, shows distinct cognitive functions are implemented by qualitatively different structures, not by rescaling one template. The convolutional neural network is the field's own proof: local receptive fields and hierarchical depth encoded this prior directly, reaching strong image recognition on far less data than later architectures needed. The paper traces how this lesson was discarded: the "Hardware Lottery" made the Transformer the path of least resistance, not the principled choice, and Mixture-of-Experts, often cited as diversity, in fact partitions parameters among identical experts. A functionalist analysis shows the Transformer is best understood as a functional analog of the hippocampal formation, not a general-purpose cortex - the same mistake as treating cortex as one giant Broca's area, except the field has now standardized on a giant hippocampus, applied to tasks it was never built for: audition, executive gating, working memory. The paper closes with an alternative: a Heterogeneous Topological Network, a System of Systems in which distinct modules keep the inductive bias their computation demands and communicate through standardized interfaces. This is a design discipline for AI architects, not cognitive science: specify modularity before training, using structural evidence as a design input rather than reverse-engineering architecture from a trained model's behavior.

</details>


### [158] [Coordinating from Memory: Graph-Structured Experience Reuse for Multi-Agent Adaptation in Dynamic Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19985)
*Chengxiao Dai,Zhanhui Lin,Zhaokun Yan,Youyang Ni,Chenjun Lei,Luyan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于动态制造环境中多智能体协调的图结构经验记忆（GSEM）框架，通过图神经网络检索历史协调经验以加速应对新扰动的策略适应，显著提升了调度性能和适应速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于多智能体强化学习的方法在每次扰动发生时都从零开始学习，忽略了历史协调经验的价值，导致在频繁扰动下适应效率低下。

Method: 提出GSEM框架，将历史协调片段编码为异构关系图，利用图神经网络进行结构相似性检索，并指导策略快速适应新的扰动场景。

Result: 在三种扰动类型的动态柔性作业车间调度基准上实验表明，相比最强的记忆增强基线，GSEM降低4.1%-10.0%的完工时间，并减少33%-38%的适应时间，且在扰动频率更高时优势更明显。

Conclusion: 图结构化编码和基于相似性的经验检索对多智能体在动态环境中的快速适应至关重要，所提方法具备跨扰动类型泛化能力，有效提升制造系统的鲁棒性与效率。

Abstract: Dynamic manufacturing environments require multi-agent systems to coordinate effectively under frequent operational disturbances such as machine failures, urgent job arrivals, and processing time variations. Existing multi-agent reinforcement learning approaches treat each disturbance episode independently, discarding valuable coordination experience that could accelerate future adaptation. In this paper, we propose a Graph-Structured Experiential Memory (GSEM) framework for multi-agent coordination in dynamic manufacturing. The framework encodes historical coordination episodes as heterogeneous relational graphs that capture task dependencies, machine states, and inter-agent collaboration patterns. When a new disturbance occurs, a graph neural network-based retrieval mechanism identifies structurally similar past episodes, enabling experience-guided policy adaptation rather than learning from scratch. Experiments on dynamic flexible job-shop scheduling benchmarks with three disturbance types show that GSEM reduces makespan by 4.1%-10.0% and adaptation time by 33%-38% compared to the strongest memory-augmented baseline, with the advantage increasing under higher disturbance frequency. Ablation studies and cross-disturbance transfer experiments further validate the necessity of graph-structured encoding and similarity-based retrieval and demonstrate the cross-disturbance generalizability of learned coordination patterns.

</details>


### [159] [CLARK: Closed-loop Learning for Adaptive Reasoning over Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19996)
*Yousef Khan,Luca Gherardini,Marco Maratea,Joel Arrais,Jose Sousa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CLARK是一个结合知识图谱、符号规则挖掘和概率推理的框架，用于提升分类任务中的自适应性和可解释性，尤其在数据分布变化和不确定性环境下表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习模型在数据分布变化时性能下降，难以处理动态信息，且缺乏对先验知识的有效整合。

Method: 基于CACTUS衍生的知识图谱，将图结构转化为LP^MLN程序，并通过符号学习迭代引入候选规则，利用概率权重学习进行校准，实现不确定下的推理与图结构优化。

Result: 在两个医疗数据集上验证表明，CLARK提升了分类性能，增强了推理的泛化能力。

Conclusion: CLARK为构建自适应、可解释、以知识驱动的分类模型提供了原则性方法。

Abstract: Machine Learning models are widely used for automating classification tasks by extracting statistical patterns from data. However, their performance deteriorates if the data distribution changes, making them ill-suited to handle uncertain and evolving information. Moreover, they provide limited support for integrating prior knowledge. To address these limitations, we present CLARK (Closed-loop Learning for Adaptive Reasoning over Knowledge Graphs), a framework that integrates knowledge graphs, symbolic rule mining, and probabilistic reasoning under the Logic Programs with Markov Logic Networks (LP$^{\text{MLN}}$) formalism. Starting from CACTUS-derived KGs, CLARK translates graph structure into an LP$^{\text{MLN}}$ program and iteratively enriches it with candidate rules proposed by symbolic learners. These rules are calibrated through probabilistic weight learning, enabling reasoning under uncertainty and refinement of the underlying graph structure. We evaluate CLARK on two medical datasets, analysing both rule quality and downstream classification performance. Results demonstrate that CLARK leads to improved classification performance and more generalisable inference. Overall, CLARK provides a principled approach to constructing adaptive, interpretable, knowledge-driven models for classification.

</details>


### [160] [Safe Remediation as Risk-Constrained Intervention Decision in Microservice Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20005)
*Chengxiao Dai,Zhaokun Yan,Chenjun Lei,Qiao Li,Luyan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种安全的自动化修复框架，通过将安全修复建模为受风险约束的干预决策问题，结合三维风险分解和自适应人机协作机制，在降低误修复率的同时提高了修复成功率并减轻了运维负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动化修复系统通常优先生成操作而忽视安全性，导致错误修复成本高昂，因此需要一种能主动评估干预必要性并确保安全性的新方法。

Method: 将安全修复问题建模为受约束的马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP），引入包含影响范围、可逆性和认知不确定性在内的三维风险分解，并设计上下文自适应的人在回路（HITL）机制；策略从历史事件日志中离线学习，以显式控制预期的误修复率（FRR）。

Result: 在Train Ticket微服务基准上进行实验，结合Chaos Mesh故障注入和RCAEval对齐的故障分类体系，结果显示该框架相比强基线运行手册方法降低了39%的误修复率，修复成功率提高2.5个百分点，并相对于固定阈值版本减少17%的值班告警升级量。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架有效平衡了自动修复的成功率与安全性，通过可解释的风险评估和自适应的人机协同机制，实现了更可靠、可控的IT运维自动化。

Abstract: In modern IT operations (IT-Ops), the cost of an incorrect repair often exceeds the cost of no action at all. Yet existing automated remediation systems are designed to generate actions rather than to decide whether intervention is warranted, leaving safety as an afterthought enforced by manual approval. This paper makes three contributions to close this gap: (i) we reformulate safe remediation as a risk-constrained intervention decision problem and cast it as a Constrained Markov Decision Process (CMDP), in which the agent maximizes repair success subject to a bounded false remediation rate (FRR); (ii) we introduce a three-dimensional risk decomposition comprising blast radius, reversibility, and epistemic uncertainty, providing operators with an interpretable per-action safety interface; and (iii) we design a context-adaptive human-in-the-loop (HITL) gate that turns escalation from a binary failsafe into a bandwidth-aware control layer responsive to on-call load and business criticality. The full policy is learned offline from historical incident logs, enabling explicit control of the expected FRR. Experiments on the Train Ticket microservice benchmark with Chaos Mesh fault injection and an RCAEval-aligned fault taxonomy show that our framework reduces FRR by 39% while improving repair success by 2.5 points over a strong runbook baseline, and reduces on-call escalation load by 17% relative to a fixed-threshold variant.

</details>


### [161] [EvoDRC: A Self-Evolving Agentic Framework for Automated DRC Violation Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20019)
*Bing-Yue Wu,Chia-Tung Ho,Haoyu Yang,Brucek Khailany,Vidya A. Chhabria*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EvoDRC是一种用于模块级设计规则检查（DRC）修复的技能进化框架，通过从参考设计中提取知识并持续积累目标设计的可追溯修复经验，实现自动化、高效的DRV修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在先进工艺节点中，设计规则检查（DRC）闭合是物理设计的主要瓶颈，尽管详细布线工具具备规则感知能力，仍常存在残余违规，需手动反复修正，自动化修复面临几何复杂性、连通性保持和避免新违规等挑战。

Method: 提出EvoDRC框架，初始化层特定修复技能，并通过目标设计中的可追溯修复经验持续进化技能；将布局分解为有限修复区域，为每个区域分配基于大语言模型的修复代理；利用局部DRC分析、连通性检查和影响预览工具提供反馈，并将修复操作及其效果存入知识库以优化技能。

Result: 在DAC26 DRC基准的七个模块级设计上实验表明，EvoDRC相比基线实现了73.5%的整体违规减少。

Conclusion: EvoDRC通过技能演化和代理式局部修复，有效实现了高精度、自动化的模块级DRC修复，显著提升物理设计流程效率。

Abstract: Design rule check (DRC) closure remains a major bottleneck in advanced-node physical design. Although detailed routers are rule-aware, residual design rule violations (DRVs) often require manual engineering change order iterations. Automating this process is challenging because repairs must account for complex geometric interactions, preserve circuit connectivity, and avoid introducing new violations. We present EvoDRC, a skill-evolution framework for agentic block-level DRC repair. EvoDRC initializes layer-specific repair skills using knowledge distilled from an unrelated reference design and continuously evolves these skills using traceable repair experience collected from the target design. EvoDRC decomposes the layout into bounded repair regions and assigns an LLM repair agent to each region. Local DRC analysis, connectivity-checking, and impact-preview tools provide feedback on proposed modifications. Repair operations and their resulting DRV changes are stored in a knowledge database and used to evolve the repair skills. Experiments on seven block-level designs from the DAC26 DRC Benchmark show that EvoDRC achieves a 73.5\% overall reduction compared to the reported baseline.

</details>


### [162] [Global Difference Constraint Propagation for Constraint Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20022)
*Lucas Kletzander,Jip J. Dekker,Andreas Schutt,Peter J. Stuckey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种全局的差分约束传播器，能够同时处理多个差分约束，相较于传统单独处理每个约束的方法，显著提升了求解效率，并解决了在惰性子句生成求解器中传播解释的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的有限域传播算法通常将每个差分约束视为独立的传播器，导致求解过程缓慢，尽管能保证完备性。因此需要一种更高效的全局处理机制。

Method: 构建一个能够同时处理所有差分约束的边界一致（bounds consistent）全局传播器，并设计方法以支持在惰性子句生成（lazy clause generation）求解器中对传播进行解释。

Result: 实验表明，采用全局方式处理差分约束可显著优于传统的逐个传播方法，大幅提高求解效率。

Conclusion: 全局差分约束传播器不仅提升了传播效率，还通过有效的解释机制，适用于现代约束求解框架，如SAT模理论和惰性子句生成求解器。

Abstract: Difference constraints of the form $x - y \leq d$ are well studied, with efficient algorithms for satisfaction and implication, because of their connection to shortest paths. Finite domain propagation algorithms, however, typically do not make use of these algorithms, and treat each difference constraint as a separate propagator. Propagation does guarantee completeness of solving, but can be needlessly slow. In this paper we describe how to build a (bounds consistent) global propagator for difference constraints that treats them all simultaneously. SAT modulo theory solvers have included theory solvers for difference constraints for some time. While a theory solver for difference constraints gives the basis of a global difference constraint propagator, we show how the requirements on the propagator are quite different. Crucially, we show how to explain propagations by a global difference constraint propagator, in order to use it within a lazy clause generation solver. We give experiments showing that treating difference constraints globally can substantially improve on the standard propagation approach.

</details>


### [163] [Reading and Steering Representations of Materials-Science Mechanisms in an Open-Weight Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20058)
*Markus J. Buehler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了大语言模型在材料科学问题中对物理机制的理解方式，发现其内部表征通过隐藏状态变化而非静态状态本身体现物理规律，并通过多种方法验证了这些表征具有因果性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型在回答科学问题时是否真正理解并运用了背后的物理规律，而不仅仅是生成正确答案。

Method: 结合直接读出与雅可比读出、无选项的状态几何分析、60条反事实基准测试以及因果干预实验，分析模型内部表示如何编码和使用材料科学中的物理机制。

Result: 在50个未见过的材料描述中，三个独立拟合的雅可比透镜成功复现概念排序；9/10种机制家族可通过读出的词集盲识别；72个提示形成的隐藏状态邻域看似有序，但图审计显示主要由数值比较驱动；方向反转实验证明状态变换遵循输入的本构关系，40个定向律中39个被正确引导；双向干预显著改变答案概率并实现决策信号跨机制迁移。

Conclusion: 大语言模型中物理关系更多体现在受控的状态变化过程中，而非绝对的隐藏状态中，表明其内部存在可分离且具因果作用的机制表征。

Abstract: Large language models can answer scientific questions, yet a correct output does not reveal whether the model represents or uses the governing physics. Here we show that materials science mechanism information in the open-weight google/gemma-4-E4B-it model has three experimentally separable forms: concepts are readable in individual hidden states, constitutive orientation is carried by controlled transformations between states, and selected internal representations causally control engineering answers. We combine matched direct and Jacobian vocabulary readouts, option-free state geometry, a 60-law counterfactual benchmark and causal interventions. In 50 held-out materials descriptions, three independently fitted Jacobian lenses reproduced concept ranks, and target-free word sets from both readouts enabled blinded identification of 9 of 10 mechanism families. A separate 72-prompt benchmark produced mechanism-specific hidden-state neighborhoods, but an exact graph audit showed that this apparent physical organization was equally explained by numerical comparison. We therefore compared otherwise identical prompts in which only the direction of the physical input was reversed, asking whether the resulting hidden-state movement followed the supplied constitutive law. These state transformations ordered direct, physically neutral and inverse laws across 60 frozen relations and correctly oriented 39 of 40 directional laws, whereas lexical controls were near chance. Bidirectional interventions shifted answer probabilities toward or away from the physically appropriate outcome across all 12 matched cases, while counterfactual state patches transferred opposing decision signals across mechanisms and answer formats. Physical relationships were therefore more visible in controlled state changes than in absolute states alone.

</details>


### [164] [PRO-LONG: Programmatic Memory Enables Long-Horizon Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20064)
*Alexis Fox,Junlin Wang,Paul Rosu,Bhuwan Dhingra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PRO-LONG是一种面向长视野任务的轻量级上下文管理框架，通过程序化记忆和结构化交互日志提升LLM代理在持续学习基准（如ARC-AGI-3）上的表现，在减少4.2-5.8倍token消耗的同时显著提高性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理在处理长视野任务时面临上下文管理困境：保留更多信息会降低关键信息检索效率，导致在持续学习和探索性任务中表现受限，尤其是在开箱即用的设置下。

Method: 提出PRO-LONG框架，维护完整且结构化的交互日志，并利用代码生成代理的能力高效搜索历史记录，从而实现对环境信息的选择性访问与利用。

Result: 在ARC-AGI-3全公开数据集上，PRO-LONG使前沿模型平均提升18.0个百分点，达到最高76.1%的pass@1成绩，且使用token仅为其他方法的1/4.2到1/5.8；结合Fable 5实现97.4%的best@2准确率，总成本1,750美元。

Conclusion: PRO-LONG通过程序化记忆有效平衡了信息保留与检索效率之间的权衡，为LLM代理在长视野、探索性任务中的上下文管理提供了高效、可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Long-horizon tasks require sustained perception, reasoning, and exploration, and are a persistent challenge for large language model (LLM) agents. This gap is reflected in their limited performance on continual learning benchmarks such as ARC-AGI-3, especially when models are evaluated out of the box. Various agent harnesses have been proposed to close this gap, and each commits to a strategy for handling long sequences of observations, i.e., what information to save from the environment and how to load it into model context, a choice we argue is particularly consequential. Existing methods for context management face a significant tradeoff, as preserving more information makes retrieving relevant details less tractable. We propose PRO-LONG, a minimal context management framework built around programmatic memory for LLM agents in long-horizon, exploratory settings. PRO-LONG addresses the tradeoff by keeping a complete, structured interaction log and capitalizing on recent progress in coding agents to search this history efficiently. On the full ARC-AGI-3 public game set, PRO-LONG improves over a base coding agent by an average of 18.0 percentage points across frontier models, and matches or exceeds state-of-the-art specialized harnesses (up to 76.1% pass@1) while using 4.2-5.8x fewer tokens. With Fable 5, PRO-LONG achieves 97.4% best@2 at a total cost of \$1,750. Relevant code and logs are available at https://github.com/alexisfox7/PRO-LONG.

</details>


### [165] [TRUST-ESD: A Risk-Calibrated and Governance-Aware AI Framework for Enterprise Strategic Decision Support Under Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20065)
*Tian Qiu,Li Yan,Mahabubur Rahman Miraj,Shanqin Yi,Md Intekhab Rahman Galib,Jahid Hasan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为TRUST-ESD的风险校准且符合治理要求的企业战略决策支持框架，通过结合不确定性估计、风险评分、可解释性和合规性验证，提升企业决策的可靠性与可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业战略决策需要不仅准确而且具备不确定性感知、风险校准、可解释性和治理合规性的AI系统，现有方法多关注预测性能而忽视风险与合规平衡。

Method: TRUST-ESD框架整合了反事实策略评估、预测效用估计、符合性不确定性校准、基于CVaR的下行风险评分、风险记忆检索、策略即代码治理、可解释性模块和人工监督机制。

Result: 相比强基线模型，TRUST-ESD提升了7.95%的风险调整效用，降低23.22%的风险暴露和23.78%的CVaR，校准误差下降13.89%，解释保真度提高10.90%，合规性提升9.76%，同时保持竞争力的预测精度。

Conclusion: 不确定性校准、下行风险评分、风险记忆、可解释性和治理验证共同增强了企业决策系统的可信度，TRUST-ESD为高风险环境下的企业决策提供了可靠支持。

Abstract: Enterprise strategic decision support requires AI systems that are not only accurate, but also uncertainty-aware, risk-calibrated, explainable, and governance-compliant. This paper proposes TRUST-ESD, a risk-calibrated and governance-aware framework for enterprise decision support under uncertainty. TRUST-ESD evaluates feasible counterfactual strategies through predictive utility estimation, conformal uncertainty calibration, CVaR-based downside-risk scoring, risk-memory retrieval, policy-as-code governance, explainability, and human oversight. Unlike prediction-only methods that select actions by maximum expected utility, TRUST-ESD recommends strategies that balance value, reliability, risk exposure, and compliance. Experimental results show that TRUST-ESD improves risk-adjusted utility by 7.95%, reduces risk exposure by 23.22%, reduces CVaR by 23.78%, lowers calibration error by 13.89%, improves explanation fidelity by 10.90%, and increases governance compliance by 9.76% compared with strong uncertainty-aware baselines, while maintaining competitive predictive accuracy. Ablation and case-study analyses further confirm that uncertainty calibration, downside-risk scoring, risk memory, explainability, and governance validation jointly improve trustworthy enterprise decision-making.

</details>


### [166] [CUSUM-Shaped Inference-Time Monitoring and Targeted Re-Decoding for Quantized Small Language Model Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20129)
*El Hassane Ettifouri,Ayoub Belfatmi,Mahaman Sanoussi Yahaya Alassan,Walid Dahhane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MGT-B方法，通过监控和回溯机制改善量化小自回归推理模型在推理过程中的重复或无效路径问题，在特定数据集上显示出准确性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化的小型自回归推理模型容易进入长而重复或无效的推理路径，传统方法在推理时无法动态调整计算资源分配，缺乏对生成轨迹的实时监控与修正机制。

Method: 基于e-CUSUM控制器，提出MGT-B（Monitoring-Guided Test-time Backtracking）外部控制器，利用滑动窗口监测采样前的不确定性与退化特征，映射到位置相关的尾部概率，结合带重置机制的CUSUM型累积混合投注因子，在触发警报时估计回滚点、恢复缓存状态并进行约束性重解码。

Result: 在240对样本的审计集中，准确率从82/240提升至88/240（+2.5个百分点），McNemar检验p=0.2632，置信区间[-1.25, +6.25]；在包含467对的历史覆盖集中，准确率从146/467升至167/467（+4.5点，p=0.000753），其中所有未触发警报的输出与原始模型一致，151条警报轨迹中有29次修正和8次退步。

Conclusion: MGT-B支持在MATH-500设定下采用选择性监控与修复机制来改进推理过程，但结果尚不构成确证性结论，也未建立理论保证，仅表明该机制在特定场景中具有潜力。

Abstract: Quantized small autoregressive reasoning models can enter long, repetitive, or unproductive trajectories, yet inference-time compute is usually allocated without observing how a trajectory develops. Building on an earlier token-level e-CUSUM controller, we develop MGT-B (Monitoring-Guided Test-time Backtracking), a revised external controller that maps overlapping windows of pre-sampling uncertainty and degeneration features to position-conditional empirical tail probabilities, accumulates mixture betting factors with a CUSUM-shaped reset, and responds to an alarm by estimating a rollback point, restoring token and key-value-cache state, and performing constrained re-decoding. To audit whether the effect persists on problem identities first observed after the manual choice of log threshold h = 10, we retrospectively exclude 260 IDs present in pre-threshold artifacts and retain the chronologically first post-threshold pair for each remaining ID, yielding a 240-pair chronology-audit set. On this set, accuracy changes from 82/240 to 88/240 (+2.50 percentage points; 13 corrections, 7 regressions; exact McNemar p = 0.2632; paired bootstrap 95% interval [-1.25, +6.25]). A broader 467-pair historical-coverage set of seed-matched pairs changes accuracy from 146/467 to 167/467 (+4.50 points; McNemar p = 0.000753), but includes 200 seed-1 IDs available before or during threshold selection and is reported only as an exploratory estimate. All 316 no-alarm outputs in the 467-pair set are identical to vanilla, while the 151 alarmed trajectories contain 29 corrections and 8 regressions. Neither analysis is confirmatory, and the empirical factors are not established as a valid e-process or e-detector. The results support a selective monitoring-and-repair mechanism for the studied MATH-500 setting, rather than a general or theoretically certified reasoning improvement.

</details>


### [167] [PoTRE: Test-Time Reasoning inspired by Cognitive Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20268)
*Anmol Kankariya,Sercan Ö. Arık*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PoTRE是一种异构推理框架，通过四种不同代理分工协作并由任务自适应聚合层整合，显著提升大模型在复杂推理任务中的表现，在多个前沿基准上达到先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在面对需要长程规划和迭代纠错的复杂推理任务时表现不佳，且传统单一流程提示方法在遇到新抽象或严格领域约束时容易失效。因此需要一种更鲁棒的推理架构。

Method: 提出PoTRE框架，将推理过程解耦为四个异构代理：对抗性精炼代理、分层战略规划代理、谱搜索代理和直接链代理，并通过任务自适应聚合层动态整合结果，支持最终候选选择、语义合成或神经符号验证。

Result: 在ARC-AGI-2、Humanity's Last Exam（HLE）和PRBench Finance三个前沿基准上进行评估，PoTRE在HLE上取得49.92%的最先进准确率，超过此前最优成绩，同时使用相似或更少的推理token。

Conclusion: 异构架构设计能有效提升复杂推理性能，相比大规模同质化模型更具效率和鲁棒性，为下一代推理系统提供了可行路径。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) excel at many tasks, they frequently struggle with complex reasoning that requires long-horizon planning and iterative error correction. Furthermore, standard single-stream prompting proves brittle when models encounter novel abstractions or rigorous domain constraints. We introduce PoTRE (Poly-Topological Reasoning Ensembles), a heterogeneous framework that decouples inference into four agents: (1) Adversarial Refinement Agent, (2) Hierarchical strategic Planning Agent, (3) Spectrum Search Agent, and (4) Direct Chain Agent. A final Task-Adaptive Aggregation Layer dynamically reconciles these perspectives -- via final candidate selection, semantic synthesis, or neuro-symbolic verification -- to produce a robust global solution. We evaluate PoTRE on three frontier benchmarks: ARC-AGI-2, Humanity's Last Exam (HLE), and PRBench Finance. PoTRE achieves state-of-the-art accuracy of 49.92% on HLE, surpassing the previous best official score. We demonstrate that this architectural heterogeneity achieves improved reasoning performance using similar or fewer inference tokens compared to heavily scaled homogeneous baselines.

</details>


### [168] [Train the Model, Not the Reader: Decodability Supervision for Verifiable Activation Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20379)
*Hiskias Dingeto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了自然语言自编码器在解释隐藏激活时的重建评估方法，发现其对个别虚假陈述缺乏敏感性，并提出RECAP方法以提高解释的可读性和真实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于重建的评估方法无法有效检测解释中的具体虚假陈述，导致解释看似合理但实际上不忠于模型行为，本文旨在解决这一问题。

Method: 通过分析Qwen-2.5-7B和Pythia-160M模型上的重建表现，设计两种审计协议（grounded-vs-true cross和evaluator swap），并提出RECAP方法——训练与目标模型协同的线性头来保持指定内容可解码。

Result: 实验显示标准重建方法依赖于少数关键陈述（约2%）且易生成私有编码；RECAP使指定内容可被独立探针可靠解码，在sandbox和Pythia模型上显著提升真实陈述识别能力（AUC 0.96 vs 0.82），并在对抗攻击下仍能有效识别谎言（AUC 0.95）。

Conclusion: 高重建分数不能保证解释中每个主张的真实性；RECAP提供了一种更安全、可验证的机制，使模型内部内容可通过独立探针检查，增强可解释性和AI安全性。

Abstract: Natural-language autoencoders score explanations of hidden activations by reconstruction: an explanation is deemed faithful if the activation can be regenerated from it. The test is structurally insensitive to individual false claims: if flipping a claim does not change the reconstruction, the claim is never penalized. We show the test is passed in two ways, neither faithful. On a released Qwen-2.5-7B verbalizer, explanations reconstruct well above chance while ~2% of specific claims are reconstruction-dependent, so the score tracks gist, not specific facts. Under exact synthetic ground truth, the standard recipe develops co-adapted private codes (false wording the reconstruction depends on) in 5/5 runs, and fixes that leave the target model unchanged do not help. We contribute two audit protocols, the grounded-vs-true cross and the evaluator swap, and RECAP (Readable Encodings via Co-trained Auxiliary Predictors): linear heads trained alongside the target model to keep designated content decodable. On RECAP-trained sandbox models, fresh verbalizers state the designated content truly and the codes vanish, at a +0.001-nat cost. This replicates on a pretrained Pythia-160M: the content becomes reliably probe-decodable, though a fresh verbalizer conveys it only in part (truth 0.44-0.46 vs a near-zero control). For interpretability, high reconstruction does not certify individual claims. For AI safety, RECAP makes designated internal content independently checkable against probes rather than asserted by prose a model can game: an independent probe scores the verbalizer's true claims above its false ones (AUC 0.96, vs 0.82 without RECAP). Against an adversary that edits an explanation to maximize the reconstruction score while lying (suppressing ~87% of its lie penalty), the RECAP probe still flags the lies (AUC 0.95) while the control probe collapses to chance (0.51).

</details>


### [169] [SoftReason: A Fully Differentiable Neuro-Soft-Symbolic Deductive Reasoning Architecture over High-Dimensional Perceptual Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20402)
*Wael AbdAlmageed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为SoftReason的神经软符号架构，用于在潜在感知事实和知识图谱提供的谓词上进行可微分演绎推理，实现了端到端的感知接地、知识图谱证据注入和可微分演绎闭包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统神经符号系统中感知与推理之间离散接口导致的梯度断层问题，并实现从高维输入中联合学习感知与逻辑推理。

Method: 设计了一个可学习的可微分即时后果算子提升方法，通过谓词定义嵌入和潜在组合通道生成软体谓词混合，聚合所有可能的见证者，提出查询条件下的头事实，并通过单调概率或操作更新解释。

Result: 在知识感知视觉问答（KVQA）任务上实例化该框架，验证了其支持端到端训练的能力，能够融合感知输出与知识图谱三元组作为软证据进行联合推理。

Conclusion: SoftReason弥合了感知与符号推理之间的鸿沟，提供了一种统一的可微分框架，使基于知识图谱的复杂推理能够在连续空间中进行端到端优化。

Abstract: In many reasoning problems, the premises are not observed as discrete symbols, but must be inferred from high-dimensional inputs. Further, the predicate vocabulary, argument structure, and trusted evidence are supplied by a Knowledge Graph (KG), or rule definitions. Classical neuro-symbolic pipelines have a discrete interface between perception and deduction. We present a neuro-soft-symbolic architecture for differentiable deductive reasoning over latent perceptual facts and knowledge-provided predicates. SoftReason removes the gradient gap by representing the deductive state as a local soft interpretation tensor over candidate constants and predicates. Perception proposes probabilistic base facts, KG triples enter as high-confidence soft evidence, and every query anchor, predicate choice, and closure update remains differentiable. Our core innovation is a learned differentiable lift of the immediate-consequence operator. It uses predicate-definition embeddings and latent composition channels to form soft body-predicate mixtures, aggregate over all possible witnesses, propose query-conditioned head facts, and update the interpretation through a monotone probabilistic OR. We instantiate the framework on Knowledge-aware Visual Question Answering (KVQA), and demonstrates how SoftReason supports end-to-end perceptual grounding, KG evidence injection, and differentiable deductive closure in one trainable architecture.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [170] [ModPack: An Extensible Teleoperation Interface for Bimanual Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19479)
*Joshua Citron,Renee Zbizika,Zeyi Liu,Shuran Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ModPack是一个模块化、可扩展的遥操作系统，通过自包含的可穿戴背包集成计算、电源、通信和存储，支持多种机器人形态和任务需求，并具备即插即用的功能模块，适用于数据采集与策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥操作系统通常针对特定机器人硬件和任务领域定制，缺乏可扩展性和适应性，限制了其广泛应用。

Method: 设计了一个名为ModPack的统一框架，核心是集成了计算、电源、通信和数据存储的可穿戴背包，支持即插即用的模块化功能（如关节级遥操作、移动操作和主动感知）。

Result: 在两个不同的机器人平台和实际移动操作任务中验证了系统的灵活性和可重用性，能够有效支持数据收集和策略学习。

Conclusion: ModPack提供了一个灵活、可扩展且开源的遥操作解决方案，适用于多样化的机器人系统和应用场景。

Abstract: Existing teleoperation systems are often tailored to specific robot hardware and task domains, limiting their scalability and adaptability. We present ModPack, a modular and extensible teleoperation system designed to support diverse robot embodiments and task requirements within a unified framework. At the core of ModPack is a self-contained wearable "backpack" that integrates onboard computation, power, communication, and data storage. Built on top of this shared interface, the system supports plug-and-play capability modules including joint-level teleoperation with haptic feedback, mobile manipulation, and active perception. Experiments across two distinct robot platforms and real-world mobile manipulation tasks demonstrate that ModPack provides a flexible and reusable framework for data collection and policy learning. To support future research, we open-source the complete hardware design and software stack. Project website: https://modpack-robotics.github.io/

</details>


### [171] [Emergent Autonomous Drifting for Collision Avoidance in Real-World Winter Driving Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19484)
*Elliot Weiss,Michael Thompson,Thomas Lew,John Subosits*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了在真实冬季驾驶条件下，漂移是否对安全有利，并提出了一种可漂移的非线性模型预测控制（MPC）系统，在高保真模拟器中验证其在避免碰撞和道路偏离场景中的有效性，结果表明该系统相比传统电子稳定控制系统（ESC）在高速下能更有效地降低车道误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的防碰撞是研究车辆大侧滑漂移动力学与控制的核心动机，但漂移的实际优势此前主要在专门设计需要漂移的场景中测试。本文旨在探索在真实冬季驾驶条件下，漂移是否以及何时可能对安全性最优。

Method: 提出一种具备漂移能力的非线性模型预测控制（MPC）系统，该系统基于交通事故致死数据设计，用于处理真实驾驶场景，并在高保真模拟器中应用于道路偏离和对向车辆碰撞避免场景。

Result: 控制器能够在后轴驶上冰面或对向车辆滑入本车道时主动发起并维持漂移操作以保持在道路上或成功避障；与传统的电子稳定控制系统（ESC）对比显示，该漂移-capable控制器可通过牺牲稳定性换取更高的可控性，实现精确操控；蒙特卡洛实验表明，在多种速度下，该控制器的中位车道误差低于ESC，且漂移行为主要出现在较高速度情况下。

Conclusion: 在真实冬季驾驶条件下，特别是在高速遇到突发路面变化时，具备漂移能力的控制策略在提升行车安全性方面具有实际优势，漂移可作为一种有效的应急操控手段。

Abstract: Real-world collision avoidance is a core motivation for studying the dynamics and control of high sideslip drifting in vehicles, yet the practical benefit of such maneuvers has so far primarily been tested in scenarios explicitly engineered to require drifting. In this work, we explore the question of if and when drifting may be optimal for safety in real-world winter driving conditions. We present a drift-capable nonlinear model predictive control (MPC) system designed to handle scenarios grounded in crash fatality data and deploy the controller in a high fidelity simulator across road departure and oncoming vehicle collision avoidance scenarios. The controller naturally initiates and sustains drifting maneuvers to stay on the road when hitting a patch of ice on the rear axle and to avoid an oncoming vehicle that has slid into its lane. Comparisons with a benchmark electronic stability control (ESC) system demonstrate how a drift-capable controller can trade off stability for controllability to precisely maneuver through dangerous winter driving scenarios. A Monte Carlo study over random ice patches further shows that the drift-capable controller achieves lower median lane error than ESC across several speeds, while revealing that drifting emerges predominantly at higher speeds.

</details>


### [172] [Milo, a Fully Autonomous Indoor/Outdoor Robotic Guide Dog](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19530)
*Florian Golemo,Joanna Wolski,Joel Ruben Antony Moniz,Christopher Pal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Milo的开源、低成本（约2000美元）机器人导盲犬平台，能够在未知环境中实现完全自主的室内外导航，具备避障和避让行人能力，并已全面开源以提升视障用户的可及性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 导盲犬价格昂贵、等待时间长且寿命有限，而现有机器人导盲犬方案依赖环境先验或外部计算，缺乏实际部署所需的自主性。因此需要一个低成本、全自主、可扩展的替代方案。

Method: 采用改装的Unitree Go2四足机器人，配备机载计算单元和传感器；构建结合体素地图与地面、障碍物、行人检测的感知系统；基于自定义鸟瞰图模拟器训练避障策略形成导航系统，并在真实场景中进行评估。

Result: Milo在真实室内外障碍路线测试中表现出优于成本图基线方法的导航平滑性和更少的用户碰撞，实现了无需环境先验知识的端到端自主导航。

Conclusion: Milo是首个开源、低成本且完全自主的机器人导盲犬平台，具备实际应用潜力，并通过开放硬件设计和软件栈促进后续研究与普及。

Abstract: Many Blind and Low-Vision (BLV) people rely on guide dogs for moment-to-moment navigation, such as staying on path and avoiding obstacles and pedestrians. However, guide dogs are expensive to acquire and maintain (approximately \$50k USD plus ongoing costs), often involve long waiting lists, and have relatively short life expectancies. While robot guide dogs offer a promising alternative, existing approaches exploring this idea suffer from several drawbacks: They often lack the autonomy required for real-world deployment, relying on prior 3D scans of the environment, external computation, or limited awareness of the handler. In this work, we present Milo, the first open-source, low-cost (approximately \$2k USD) robotic guide dog platform capable of fulfilling the basic collaborative navigation role expected of a guide dog. Milo is fully autonomous, requiring no a priori knowledge of the environment, completely self-contained with all computation performed onboard, and suitable for both indoor and outdoor navigation while avoiding obstacles and pedestrians. Our system consists of a modified Unitree Go2 robot (equipped with onboard compute, sensors, and a handle), a perception stack combining voxel mapping with floor, obstacle, and pedestrian detection, and a navigation stack based on an obstacle-avoidance policy trained in a custom bird's-eye-view simulator. We evaluate Milo in real indoor and outdoor obstacle courses and compare it against a costmap-based baseline, demonstrating smoother navigation and fewer handler collisions. To maximize accessibility for BLV users, we release both the robot hardware instructions and the complete software stack as open source.

</details>


### [173] [Learning Personalized Safety Interventions for Haptic Human-Robot Shared Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19534)
*Dawei Zhang,Roberto Tron*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种从触觉反馈中学习用户安全偏好的框架（LfH），通过可微分控制屏障函数层自动调整安全参数，实现个性化的机器人共享控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有触觉引导系统采用预定义干预策略，难以适应不同用户和场景的安全偏好差异，需减少手动调试并实现个性化安全干预。

Method: 构建基于可微分控制屏障函数（CBF）的优化层，从稀疏的触觉示范中学习用户偏好的安全干预方式，间接调整安全参数以匹配示范行为。

Result: 仿真与实物实验表明，该框架能从少量用户输入中学习个性化安全干预策略，并显著降低生成触觉反馈与用户示范之间的不匹配。

Conclusion: 所提LfH框架能够有效实现个性化安全意图建模，提升人机共享控制中的交互自然性与安全性。

Abstract: Haptic feedback provides an implicit channel for communicating safety intentions during human-robot shared control. Existing haptic guidance systems typically employ predefined intervention strategies that cannot accommodate the diverse safety preferences of individual users or application scenarios. To address this limitation, we propose a Learning from Haptics (LfH) framework that learns user-preferred safety interventions from sparse demonstrations, eliminating the need for manual trial-and-error design. Our framework is built on a differentiable Control Barrier Function (CBF)-based optimization layer that automatically adjusts the underlying safety parameters to match the demonstrated haptic responses. Instead of tuning controller parameters directly, users teach the system how they expect it to intervene during teleoperation. The resulting haptic guidance reflects the demonstrated intervention preferences while preserving the intuitive interaction of haptic shared control. Simulation and hardware experiments demonstrate that the proposed framework can learn personalized safety interventions from sparse user input and reduce the mismatch between the generated haptic feedback and the demonstrated preferences.

</details>


### [174] [LENS: LLM-guided Environment Simplification for Planning and Control in Clutter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19633)
*Aileen Liao,Rachel Holladay,Dinesh Jayaraman,Michael Posa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LENS是一种自动生动生成场景和任务特定的动态抽象表示方法，通过合并或剪枝场景实体来简化复杂杂乱环境，提升现有规划与控制方法在多物体杂乱场景中的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器人操作方法在处理真实世界多物体杂乱场景时面临复杂性高、接触物理不可预测、干扰物多和任务模糊等问题，且当前的场景抽象依赖人工设计，难以扩展和调整。

Method: 提出LLM-guided Environment Simplification (LENS)，利用大语言模型指导环境简化，在闭环中根据任务进展动态合并或剪枝场景实体，生成去杂化的抽象场景表示，并可与现有规划与控制框架结合。

Result: 实验表明，LENS在多种高度杂乱的操作场景中，能够提升经典规划、基于模型的控制以及视觉-语言-动作模型的性能。

Conclusion: LENS提供了一种即插即用的解决方案，能自动生成适应性场景抽象，有效 bridging 仿真到现实的差距，具有良好的通用性和易用性。

Abstract: Despite recent advances in general-purpose robotic manipulation, real-world multi-object clutter remains challenging to handle for today's prevalent approaches. The problem scales in complexity due to more objects and collisions, more unpredictable contact physics, distractors, and task ambiguity. Bridging this gap to real-world deployment requires effective scene abstractions; yet today, producing such abstractions requires extensive task-specific manual engineering, which does not scale. These abstractions are costly to generate and difficult to adjust or fine-tune. We instead propose a plug-and-play fix to automatically generate scene-specific, task-specific, adaptively updating abstractions on top of existing planning and control stacks. LLM-guided Environment Simplification (LENS) produces a de-cluttered abstracted scene representation by merging (e.g., stacked objects) or pruning (e.g., distant objects) scene entities in a closed loop in response to task progress. These dynamic, task-relevant abstractions are versatile and easy to use. In our experiments, we show that LENS improves classical planning, model-based control, and a vision-language-action model, across a diverse set of highly cluttered manipulation scenes. Project website: https://lens-2026.github.io/.

</details>


### [175] [Remote ID Spoofing-Aware Trajectory Planning for Small Unmanned Aerial Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19650)
*Jeremiah Webb,Bryce Bjorkman,Abel Diaz Gonzalez,Austin Coursey,Noah Dahle,Kailani Lemieux Mack,Filippos Fotiadis,Gautam Biswas,Bryan C. Ward,Abenezer Taye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种去中心化的、防欺骗的轨迹规划框架，用于在远程识别（RID）位置欺骗攻击下运行的小型无人机系统，通过物理层观测评估广播可信度，并结合机会约束公式实现风险受限的避碰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹规划方法通常假设RID广播信息可信，但在遭遇位置欺骗攻击时可能导致飞行器间距丧失甚至空中碰撞，因此需要一种能够检测并应对RID欺骗的安全规划机制。

Method: 利用邻近飞行器的接收信号强度测量来检测欺骗行为，并概率性定位欺骗源；将由此产生的不确定性转化为基于机会约束的风险受限不安全区域，并集成到每个代理的马尔可夫决策过程规划器中，实现去中心化实时避障。

Result: 在多无人机包裹投递场景的仿真中，相比假设RID数据真实的规划器，该方法显著减少了近似空中碰撞事件，同时保持了适合实时执行的计算效率。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效提升了无人机系统在面临RID位置欺骗攻击时的安全性与鲁棒性，支持去中心化、实时运行且具备任务兼容性和可扩展性。

Abstract: This work presents a decentralized, spoofing-aware trajectory planning framework for small unmanned aerial systems operating under Remote Identification (RID) location spoofing attacks. Existing planners typically assume RID broadcasts are trustworthy, which can increase the risk of loss of separation and mid-air collisions when spoofing occurs. In contrast, the proposed approach explicitly treats RID information as unverified and incorporates physical-layer observations to assess broadcast credibility. Received signal-strength measurements from neighboring aircraft are used to detect spoofing and probabilistically localize a spoofing agent. The resulting uncertainty is converted into a risk-bounded unsafe region using a chance-constrained formulation and integrated into a per-agent Markov decision process-based planner. This enables real-time, decentralized collision avoidance while preserving mission objectives and scalability. Simulation results in a multi-aircraft package delivery scenario demonstrate reduced near mid-air collision events compared to planners that assume truthful RID data, while maintaining computational efficiency suitable for real-time execution.

</details>


### [176] [NavVerse: Benchmarking Indoor-to-Outdoor Embodied Navigation in Continuous Robot Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19695)
*Junzhe Wu,Yue Hu,Zeyu Han,Po-Hsun Chang,Yinan Dong,Behrad Rabiei,Maani Ghaffari*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NavVerse是一个面向室内到室外具身导航的物理仿真基准，涵盖100个室内场景、50个城市室外场景和50个过渡场景，包含10,000个任务实例，评估代理在跨环境导航中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导航基准多将室内与室外导航分开评估，且常忽略机器人执行过程中的实际挑战，导致对边界穿越、环境适应和运动学失败等问题研究不足。

Method: 构建包含多种场景和任务类型的物理仿真环境NavVerse，设计涵盖Object Navigation、Vision-and-Language Navigation和Place Navigation的任务，并通过可执行接口评估代理在任务成功率、路径效率和安全性方面的表现。

Result: 实验表明，当前方法在跨环境导航上仍有显著差距：端到端视觉语言动作模型（VLA）零样本表现最佳，模块化方法在安全性方面最优；从室外到室内外过渡场景中，PlaceNav任务性能明显下降，显示环境适应仍是主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: NavVerse为具身智能体在复杂真实环境中跨场景导航提供了更全面的评估平台，揭示了当前方法在适应性和安全性方面的不足，推动未来研究关注连续环境下的鲁棒导航。

Abstract: Robots deployed in delivery, campus, and emergency-response settings often need to navigate from buildings to streets within a single continuous episode. Existing benchmarks usually evaluate indoor and outdoor navigation separately, and many abstract away robot execution, leaving exit finding, boundary traversal, adaptation, and kinodynamic failures underexplored. We introduce NavVerse, a physics-enabled benchmark for indoor-to-outdoor embodied navigation. NavVerse contains 100 indoor scenes, 50 urban outdoor scenes, and 50 indoor-to-outdoor scenes, and 10,000 episodes spanning Object Navigation, Vision-and-Language Navigation, and Place Navigation tasks, where agents search for semantic points of interest such as restaurants or banks. Agents are evaluated through executable robot interfaces using task-success, path-efficiency, and safety metrics. Zero-shot experiments with RL, VLA, and modular baselines show that current agents remain far from solving cross-context navigation: end-to-end VLAs obtain the highest zero-shot success, while the modular method provides the strongest safety profile. PlaceNav further reveals a clear drop from outdoor to indoor-to-outdoor scenes, indicating that adaptation remains major bottleneck.

</details>


### [177] [Contact-Persistent Full Actuation for Aerial Physical Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19708)
*Abhimanyu Khadga,Abhinav Sinha,Shashi Ranjan Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于持续接触操作下无人机全驱动性的控制理论框架，通过引入残余力矩集和权威裕度，改进了传统的基于秩的全驱动性判据，并验证了在不同倾斜角度下保持接触操作能力的关键条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在空中物理交互任务中，传统基于控制分配矩阵秩条件或自由飞行跟踪性能的全驱动性认证可能不充分，因为部分可用广义力被交互任务消耗，仅剩余部分可用于稳定和机动，因此需要更精确的评估方法。

Method: 结合R³×SO(3)上的刚体模型与依赖于构型的广义力映射，建模多种多旋翼结构；定义在执行器限制下的可行广义力集、任务负载下的残余广义力集，以及残余权威裕度，并提出接触持续全驱动性的新判据。

Result: 证明了接触持续全驱动性等价于任务广义力位于约束可行广义力多面体内点，且残余权威半径即为到多面体边界的距离；提出了适用于不可行和边界情况的带符号残余裕度证书、松弛最大化分配证书及鲁棒可实现条件；数值仿真表明全行秩不足以保证可行接触操作，中等倾角可在推力任务中保持残余权威，而过小或过大倾角则失败。

Conclusion: 传统的全驱动性判据在接触任务中不足，必须考虑任务加载下的残余控制能力；所提框架提供了更严格的分析工具和安全滤波机制，可用于设计具备可靠物理交互能力的无人机系统。

Abstract: Fully actuated unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) are usually certified through rank conditions on a control-allocation matrix or through free-flight tracking performance. For aerial physical interaction, this certification may be incomplete. During sustained contact, part of the available wrench is consumed by the interaction task, and only the residual wrench remains available for stabilization, disturbance rejection, and maneuvering. This paper introduces a control-theoretic framework for \emph{contact-persistent full actuation}. A rigid-body model on $\R^{3}\times\SO\left(3\right)$ is combined with a morphology-dependent wrench map that captures fixed-tilt, variable-tilt, coaxial, and overactuated multirotor architectures. We define feasible wrench sets under actuator limits, residual wrench sets under task loading, and residual authority margins that strengthen the usual rank-based notion of full actuation. The main result shows that contact-persistent full actuation is equivalent to interiority of the task wrench in the constrained feasible wrench polytope, and that the residual authority radius is exactly the distance to the polytope boundary. We further introduce a signed residual-margin certificate for infeasible and boundary cases, a slack-maximizing allocation certificate, and a robust implementability condition that can be used as a margin-aware safety filter. Numerical evaluation on an abstract tilted hexarotor shows that full row rank alone does not imply feasible contact operation. Intermediate tilt angles preserve residual authority during pushing, whereas small or excessive tilts fail because of lateral-force deficiency or hover-margin loss.

</details>


### [178] [Morphing MILR: Design and control of a cable-driven limbless robot with rolling joints for maneuvering in complex environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19714)
*Donoven Dortilus,Tianyu Wang,Galen Tunnicliffe,Matthew Fernandez,Daniel I. Goldman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种可重构形态和柔顺性的绳驱动无肢机器人，实现多种运动模式的平滑切换，适用于复杂环境中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决单一形态的无肢机器人难以兼顾多地形适应性与柔顺性驱动鲁棒性的问题，实现单个机器人具备多样化的运动能力。

Method: 采用分布式绳索驱动产生行进波，结合可编程被动柔顺性和滚动关节实现弯曲平面重定向与形态锁定，无需高带宽反馈即可完成接触丰富的运动控制。

Result: 实现了侧向蜿蜒、侧滚、滚动和扭转等多种运动模式，实验验证了在障碍物丰富环境中可靠步态生成与模式切换能力。

Conclusion: 该平台通过整合可编程柔顺性与形态控制，为搜索救援、环境监测和检测等复杂环境任务提供了具有高适应性的无肢机器人解决方案。

Abstract: Limbless robots offer exceptional mobility in confined and cluttered environments due to their slender bodies and their ability to exploit body-terrain interactions. Recent designs incorporating compliance demonstrate robust locomotion without complex sensing or control; however, these systems typically rely on fixed body configurations, with each morphology specialized for a single locomotion mode or environment. This raises a key challenge: how can a single limbless robot achieve versatile locomotion while preserving the robustness of compliance-mediated locomotion? To address this challenge, we present a cable-driven limbless robot that reconfigures body morphology and compliance to enable diverse locomotion modes. Distributed cable actuation generates traveling body waves, while programmable passive compliance enables robust contact-rich locomotion without terrain knowledge or high-bandwidth feedback. Rolling joints reorient bending planes along the body, enabling rapid reconfiguration and smooth transitions between locomotion styles, and incorporate geared locking to maintain configuration without continuous power. By combining programmable bending compliance and morphology control, the platform achieves lateral undulation, sidewinding, rolling, and twisting within a single system. Experiments demonstrate reliable gait generation, traversal in obstacle-rich environments, and transitions between modes, establishing a versatile limbless platform for navigating complex environments with applications in search and rescue, environmental monitoring, and inspection.

</details>


### [179] [EgoRecovery: Acquiring Failure Recovery Ability Through Human Recovery Demonstration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19745)
*Zuhao Ge,Yuchen Zhou,Weitao Zhou,Minglei Li,Xinyu Li,Chao Wu,Hanwen Zhao,Haotian Wang,Zuxuan Wu,Xiaosong Jia,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出EgoRecovery，利用第一人称视角的人类恢复行为数据来高效训练机器人恢复策略，通过共享的修正意图空间对齐人类与机器人数据，显著提升真实世界任务中从失败中恢复的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非结构化的真实环境中，机器人需要具备从失败中恢复的能力，但通过遥操作收集足够的机器人恢复数据成本高、难以扩展，尤其是面对多样的故障模式时。

Method: 提出EgoRecovery框架，利用人类第一人称视角的恢复行为数据，将其与少量机器人恢复数据共同训练，将人类和机器人的修正行为映射到一个共享的紧凑修正意图空间，并通过学习恢复门控机制在适当时机触发修正动作。

Result: 实验表明，相比仅使用机器人数据、直接联合训练或直接意图迁移方法，EgoRecovery在真实世界的恢复任务中显著提高了从失败状态恢复的成功率，且人类每小时产生的有效恢复数据量是机器人遥操作的10倍以上。

Conclusion: 利用人类第一人称恢复数据结合小样本机器人数据，可通过共训练框架高效构建鲁棒的机器人恢复能力，解决了恢复数据稀缺与多样化故障模式下的可扩展性问题。

Abstract: Robust embodied robots should be able to recover from failures and retry tasks in order to operate reliably in unstructured and noisy real-world environments. Achieving this capability requires training policies on data that captures recovery behaviors. However, collecting such data through robot teleoperation is difficult to scale, as it is time-consuming to induce diverse failure states, perform corrective actions, and reset the environment. This challenge is further exacerbated by the high diversity of failure modes, which demands substantially more recovery data than success demonstrations. In this work, we show that egocentric human data capturing failure recovery processes provides a scalable alternative. By efficiently arranging task-level failure configurations and recording short recovery segments, human operators can generate more than 10x as much valid recovery data per hour compared to robot teleoperation under our protocol. To address the embodiment gap between human and robot, we propose EgoRecovery, a co-training framework for learning recovery behavior, where human recovery demonstrations are aligned to a compact corrective-intent space shared with robot data, which captures the timing and magnitude of correction. Only a small number of robot recovery demonstrations are required to connect this intent to executable robot actions. At deployment, a learned recovery gate predicts when correction is needed from robot observations and activates the corrective intent only in recovery states. Experiments on real-world recovery tasks show that EgoRecovery improves success from failure starts over robot-only recovery, direct co-training with human recovery data, and direct intent-transfer baselines.

</details>


### [180] [Defer to Plan: Adaptive Multi-Agent Fusion for End-to-End V2X Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19774)
*Nuoran Li,Zhang Zhang,Yueran Zhao,Tianze Wang,Chao Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种端到端的协作驾驶系统，通过优化规划任务性能，在V2X-AD中实现了优于现有方法的驾驶表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协同感知方法仅优化模块级感知能力，未能有效服务于最终的规划与控制任务。

Method: 采用MotionNetwork融合历史时序信息，利用注意力机制压缩空间特征，并通过自回归解码器自适应融合多智能体特征，引入MoE架构增强模型对异构特征的表征能力。

Result: 在闭环评估中驾驶得分为79.72，相比最先进的CoDriving基线（77.15）提升了3.33%，同时保持通信效率。

Conclusion: 所提出的端到端协作驾驶系统能更有效地提升实际驾驶性能，为V2X-AD提供了更优解决方案。

Abstract: Vehicle-to-everything-aided autonomous driving (V2X-AD) significantly enhances driving performance through information sharing. However, existing collaborative perception methods only optimize module-level perception capabilities and fail to effectively serve the ultimate planning and control tasks. We propose an end-to-end collaborative driving system that directly optimizes planning task performance. The system employs MotionNetwork to fuse historical temporal information, utilizes attention mechanisms to efficiently compress spatial features into compact tokens, and adaptively fuses multi-agent features through an autoregressive decoder. Additionally, we introduce Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture to enhance the model's representation capacity for heterogeneous features. Experiments demonstrate that our method achieves a driving score of 79.72, surpassing the state-of-the-art CoDriving baseline (77.15) by 3.33% in closed-loop evaluation while maintaining communication efficiency.

</details>


### [181] [V2F: Vision-Informed Grasp Force Prediction for Damage-Aware Robotic Handling of Date Fruits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19804)
*Shahd Shami,Obadah Wali,Eric Feron,Shinkyu Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种视觉驱动的抓取力预测框架（V2F），用于机器人安全抓取易损的椰枣果实，结合视觉特征与物理模型预测安全抓力，实验验证有效且无损伤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对椰枣果实脱离力高而抗压阈值低的特点，传统抓取方法易造成损伤，需实现安全、非接触式的抓取力预测。

Method: 通过力学表征确定安全抓取范围，构建融合计算机视觉分割、主动轮廓优化和几何特征提取的Vision-to-Force（V2F）流程，并设计基于Hertz接触方程增强的残差神经网络进行抓力预测。

Result: 模型在未见品种上的R²≈0.7，实验显示预测力可实现稳定抓取，残余变形小于1mm，无可见损伤。

Conclusion: 该视觉驱动方法可替代缓慢且具损伤风险的触觉探索，提升脆弱水果自动化处理的安全性与效率。

Abstract: This paper presents a vision-informed grasp force prediction framework for robotic handling of date fruits. Addressing the dual challenge of high detachment forces and low bruise thresholds, we first conduct mechanical characterization on date samples to define a safe grasping envelope and quantify the relationship between fruit geometry and bioyield stress. In this work, we develop a Vision-to-Force (V2F) pipeline that combines computer vision-based segmentation, active-contour refinement, and geometric feature extraction with a physics-informed residual neural network that augments a Hertz contact equation. The resulting model maps non-contact visual descriptors and cultivar metadata to predict a safe grasp force with mean validation performance of $R^2 \approx 0.7$ across unseen cultivar groups, which is a good result given the inherent mechanical variability of biological tissue. Experimental validation using a gripper and load cell indicates that the predicted forces enable stable manipulation of different types of date fruits, with residual deformations below 1 mm and no observable damage. These results show that pre-emptive, vision-driven force estimation% can replace slow and potentially damaging tactile exploration , enabling safer robotic handling of fragile fruits.

</details>


### [182] [Clinical Pathways as Safety Specifications for Physical AI in Hospital Wards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19827)
*Gabriele Franchini,Giulio Mallardi,Michele De Carolis,Filippo Lanubile*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将临床路径作为具身医疗AI系统运行时安全规范的新方法，通过整合可穿戴传感器、智能医疗设备和辅助机器人组件，实现医院环境中实时安全监控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实医院环境中，物理AI系统的安全性至关重要，尤其是在存在脆弱患者、医护人员、医疗设备和辅助机器人的情况下，需要确保系统的安全运行。

Method: 提出一种概念性机器人架构，核心是运行时安全监视器（RSM），结合时间预测、不确定性感知推理和基于约束的验证，利用来自临床路径的安全约束对多模态生理和系统信号进行评估。

Result: RSM能够检测三类事件：偏离规定护理的生理变化、硬件与通信故障以及潜在的数据篡改或滥用。

Conclusion: 该工作通过将领域特定的临床知识转化为可执行的安全约束，连接了基于学习的感知与运行时安全监控，有助于在真实医院环境中辅助护理人员，推动安全物理AI的发展。

Abstract: Ensuring safety in Physical AI systems operating in real-world environments is a critical challenge, particularly in hospital wards where vulnerable patients, clinical staff, medical devices, and assistive robots coexist. In this paper, we reinterpret Clinical Pathways as explicit runtime safety specifications for embodied medical AI. We propose a conceptual robotic architecture that integrates wearable sensors, smart medical devices, and assistive robotic components into a unified framework for real-time safety monitoring. At its core, a Runtime Safety Monitor (RSM) evaluates multimodal physiological and system-level signals against clinically defined constraints derived from the prescribed care process. Rather than relying solely on statistical anomaly detection, the proposed approach combines temporal prediction, uncertainty-aware reasoning, and constraint-based verification to identify safety violations. The RSM targets three classes of events: physiological deviations from prescribed care, hardware and communication failures, and potential data tampering or misuse. This work contributes to Safe Physical AI by operationalizing domain-specific clinical knowledge as enforceable safety constraints, bridging learning-based perception and runtime safety monitoring to assist nursing staff in real-world hospital wards.

</details>


### [183] [SOPD-SocialNav: Selective On-Policy Distillation for Vision-Language Social Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19850)
*Xinyu Zhang,Zishuo Wang,Ling Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种选择性策略蒸馏方法SOPD-SocialNav，用于将大型视觉语言模型的社会导航知识迁移到轻量级模型中，提升资源受限机器人平台上的社会感知导航能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型难以部署在资源受限的机器人平台上，而轻量级模型又缺乏足够的社会推理能力，因此需要一种有效的方法来平衡模型大小与社会导航性能。

Method: 提出了SOPD-SocialNav，采用基于熵的token选择机制，利用教师模型的不确定性识别具有社会信息的关键决策token，并通过温度控制的JS散度对齐师生模型在选定token上的分布。

Result: 在SNEI和MUSON基准上，SOPD在动作预测、感知一致性和推理一致性方面均优于监督微调、离策略蒸馏和标准策略蒸馏方法；在Scout Mini机器人上的实际部署表明其能生成更符合社交规范的导航行为。

Conclusion: SOPD是一种有效的轻量化、 socially-aware 视觉语言模型导航系统构建策略。

Abstract: Vision-language models have shown strong potential for social robot navigation by leveraging rich semantic understanding of complex environments and human behaviors. However, large scale VLMs are difficult to deploy on resource-constrained robotic platforms, while lightweight VLMs often lack sufficient social reasoning capability. To address this problem, we propose SOPD-SocialNav, a selective on-policy distillation (SOPD) method that transfers social navigation knowledge from a large teacher VLM to a lightweight student VLM. SOPD introduces an entropy-based token selection mechanism that uses teacher uncertainty to identify socially informative decision tokens, while suppressing gradients from low-entropy tokens corresponding to trivial navigation states. A temperature-controlled Jensen-Shannon divergence objective is then used to align the student and teacher distributions on the selected tokens. Experiments on the SNEI and MUSON benchmarks demonstrate that SOPD consistently outperforms supervised fine-tuning, off-policy distillation, and standard on-policy distillation baselines in action prediction, perception consistency, and reasoning consistency. Real-world deployment on a Scout Mini robot further shows that the distilled model can generate more socially appropriate navigation behaviors in conversational and queuing scenarios. These results suggest that SOPD is an effective strategy for building lightweight yet socially aware VLM-based navigation systems.

</details>


### [184] [KineBench: Benchmarking Embodied World Models via IDM-Free Kinematic Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19876)
*Zeyu Liu,Zhangzhe Zhu,Yang Zhang,Chenyou Fan,Chenjia Bai,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出KineBench，一种无需逆动力学模型（IDM）的具身世界模型（EWM）闭环评估基准，通过视觉基础模型直接提取6D末端执行器位姿，并结合物理仿真与3D运动学指标实现更可靠的物理一致性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EWM评估依赖逆动力学模型（IDM）进行动作提取，但在面对新物体和场景时易因数据分布外泛化问题导致动作提取不可靠，造成世界模型性能与提取器误差之间的归因模糊。

Method: 构建KineBench，采用级联视觉基础模型从生成视频帧中直接提取6D末端执行器位姿，送入物理引擎进行闭环验证；引入SPARC和Maruyama可操作性指数等3D运动学指标评估轨迹平滑性与运动学可行性。

Result: 在ManiSkill3的20个操作任务上验证，涵盖基础执行、任务迁移、视觉OOD泛化和复杂度缩放四个层级；发现具身视频生成性能随任务复杂度呈现非线性缩放规律。

Conclusion: KineBench有效降低了IDM带来的归因歧义，提供了更可靠、细粒度的EWM物理一致性评估框架，揭示了任务复杂度对模型表现的关键影响，为未来数据扩展策略提供实证依据。

Abstract: Evaluating the physical consistency of embodied world models(EWMs) is a critical open challenge. While closed-loop evaluation via simulator rollouts offers a more faithful assessment of physical plausibility than open-loop alternatives, existing frameworks almost exclusively rely on Inverse Dynamics Models(IDMs) for action extraction. Due to the intricate mapping from 2D pixel space to 3D kinematic space, the learned IDMs can be brittle to data outside their training distribution, resulting in unreliable action extraction from the generated videos with novel objects and scenarios. This creates an unavoidable attribution ambiguity between world model inaccuracies and extractor errors. To reduce this ambiguity, we present KineBench, an IDM-free closed-loop benchmark for EWMs, built upon an explicit kinematic grounding pipeline. Given a generated video, KineBench employs cascaded visual foundation models to directly extract 6D end-effector poses from individual frames, which are then executed in a physics simulator for closed-loop validation. Beyond execution-based task success, KineBench incorporates two classical 3D kinematic metrics--Spectral Arc Length (SPARC) and the Maruyama Manipulability Index--to characterize trajectory smoothness and kinematic feasibility from a robot-centric perspective. Built on 20 diverse manipulation tasks in ManiSkill3, KineBench evaluates EWMs across four progressive suites: basic execution, task transfer, visual out-of-distribution generalization, and complexity-conditioned scaling. Evaluation across frontier models reveals task-complexity-bounded nonlinear scaling in embodied video generation, providing empirical guidance for future data-scaling strategies.

</details>


### [185] [EA-Nav: Learning Safe Visual Navigation Policies with Embodiment Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19880)
*Jialu Zhang,Yong Du,Xianda Guo,Shunwang Sun,Xinqi Liu,Yue Sun,Guodong Lu,Wei Sui,Jituo Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于模仿学习的具身感知导航框架，通过多阶段模块化设计和体现几何信息的引入，提升跨具身导航性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有强化学习方法在跨具身导航中依赖大量交互和奖励设计、难以扩展到预训练和现实适应的问题，同时弥补模仿学习方法在此领域的不足。

Method: 构建来自互联网视频的跨具身导航数据集，在预训练中引入体现几何作为条件令牌以减少动作歧义；采用解耦架构的多模态信息注入机制，并设计轨迹增强策略生成高风险样本，分别训练空间感知与风险意识校正。

Result: 实验结果表明所提方法在不同具身设置下显著提升了导航性能，验证了引入体现几何信息对具身导航的有效性。

Conclusion: 将体现几何信息显式融入模仿学习框架，可有效支持跨具身导航的可扩展预训练与安全导航，为具身智能提供了新思路。

Abstract: Cross-embodiment navigation is a key challenge in embodied intelligence. Due to differences in embodiment, the same visual observation may imply different actions for different agents, making prediction ambiguous when relying solely on vision. Existing studies mainly rely on reinforcement learning, which requires large-scale interaction and careful reward design, making it difficult to support scalable pretraining and real-world adaptation. In contrast, imitation-learning-based approaches remain limited. To address these challenges, we propose an imitation-learning-based embodiment-aware navigation framework with a modular multi-stage design. In pretraining, we construct a cross-embodiment navigation dataset from Internet videos and introduce embodiment geometry as conditional tokens to reduce action ambiguity under the same observation. In fine-tuning, we design a multimodal information injection mechanism based on a decoupled architecture. Specifically, we design a trajectory augmentation strategy to generate high-risk samples, which are used to train spatial perception and risk-aware correction separately, thereby explicitly incorporating embodiment geometry for safe navigation.
